### Q1. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>B</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo></math> then the ordered pair <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mi>B</mi><mo>)</mo></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4, 5</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>B</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

Put <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>⇒</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>⇒</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></math>

<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

Put <math><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><mi>∞</mi><mo>⇒</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>⇒</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math>

</details>

### Q2. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>. If the maximum value of <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>k</mi><mo>,</mo></math> then <math><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>k</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **49**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mrow><mi>θ</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn></math>

</details>

### Q3. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi></math> be a matrix of order <math><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>3</mn></math> such that <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>.</mo></math> Let <math><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math> and <math><mi>C</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>j</mi><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>, then the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>A</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi>B</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><msup><mi>C</mi><mn>4</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>16</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>14</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>16</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn></math>
<math><mi>C</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>j</mi><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>A</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi>B</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><msup><mi>C</mi><mn>4</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>A</mi></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>B</mi></mfenced><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>C</mi></mfenced><mn>4</mn></msup></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#8901;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#8901;</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>16</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q4. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#946;</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#947;</mi></math> are the roots of the equation <math><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>, then the value of <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>&#947;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>32</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>16</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>56</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>64</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mn>56</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi>α</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>β</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>γ</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math> 

 <math><mi>α</mi><mi>β</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>β</mi><mi>γ</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>γ</mi><mi>α</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math> 

 <math><mi>α</mi><mi>β</mi><mi>γ</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></math> 

 <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>β</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>γ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>β</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>γ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>γ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi>β</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math> 

 <math><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>β</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>γ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>β</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>γ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi>γ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi>β</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math> 

 <math><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>β</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>γ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>β</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>γ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>β</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>γ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>β</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>γ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi>γ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi>β</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math> 

 <math><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>β</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>β</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>γ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>γ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>α</mi><mi>β</mi><mi>γ</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math> 

 <math><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>α</mi><mi>β</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>β</mi><mi>γ</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>γ</mi><mi>α</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>α</mi><mi>β</mi><mi>γ</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>α</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>β</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>γ</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>α</mi><mi>β</mi><mi>γ</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math> 

 <math><mo>=</mo><mn>16</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>16</mn></math> 

 <math><mo>=</mo><mn>56</mn></math>

</details>

### Q5. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Consider the matrices <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mi>B</mi><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mi>C</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>D</mi></math> with order <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mn>4</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mn>3</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>3</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>2</mn></math> respectively. Let <math><mi>M</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><msup><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2020</mn></mrow></msup><mi>D</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></math> and <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>M</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>λ</mi><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><msup><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2020</mn></mrow></msup><mi>D</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></math>, then <math><mi>λ</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>1024</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>4096</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>64</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>4096</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi>M</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><msup><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2020</mn></mrow></msup><mi>D</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></math> (Given)
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>M</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><msup><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2020</mn></mrow></msup><mi>D</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></math>
<math><mo>∵</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><msup><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2020</mn></mrow></msup><mi>D</mi></math> is a <math><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>2</mn></math> matrix
Hence, <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><msup><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2020</mn></mrow></msup><mi>D</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><msup><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2020</mn></mrow></msup><mi>D</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
Therefore, 
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>M</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><msup><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2020</mn></mrow></msup><mi>D</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>λ</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4096</mn></math>

</details>

### Q6. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi></math> are two non-singular matrices of order <math><mn>3</mn></math> such that <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>A</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi>B</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>O</mi></math>, then the value of <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to (where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>O</mi></math> is the null matrix of order <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn></math>)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><msup><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>A</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi>B</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>O</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mi>A</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mi>B</mi><mi>B</mi><msup><mi>B</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><msup><mi>B</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mi>O</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mi>A</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mi>B</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>O</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>A</mi><msup><mi>A</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mi>B</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>A</mi><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>O</mi><mspace linebreak="newline"/><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>A</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></math>
Hence, <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi></mi><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>5</mn><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>.</mo><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mi></mi></math>

</details>

### Q7. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> is an invertible matrix of order <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math> is another matrix of the same order as of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>,</mo></math> such that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></msup><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mfenced><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></msup><mo>.</mo></math> If <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mo>,</mo></math> then the value of <math><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **0.25**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mo>&#8757;</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow></msup><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow></mfenced><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow></msup></math>
Taking determinant on both sides, we get, 
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></msup></mfenced><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mfenced><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mfenced><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mfenced><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></msup></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
Now, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>|</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>|</mo><mo>&#215;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>|</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>|</mo></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>|</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>(</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>|</mo></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#215;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>16</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q8. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and <math><mi>a</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> have a non-trivial solution, then the number of real value(s) of <math><mi>a</mi></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>3</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>1</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>For non-trivial solution, using Cramer&#39;s rule, we get,
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>a</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>a</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mn>4</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>16</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math></p><p>Hence, only one value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q9. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>a</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>b</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>, then <math><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>b</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>8</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>5</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mn>8</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mo>∴</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>⇒</mo><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mi>I</mi></math>
<math> <mrow>  <msup>   <mi>A</mi>   <mn>2</mn>  </msup>  <mo>=</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>&#x22C5;</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[">   <mrow>    <mtable equalrows='true' equalcolumns='true'>     <mtr>      <mtd>       <mrow>        <mo>&#x2212;</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow>      </mtd>      <mtd>       <mrow>        <mo>&#x2212;</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow>      </mtd>      <mtd>       <mrow>        <mo>&#x2212;</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow>      </mtd>     </mtr>     <mtr>      <mtd>       <mn>1</mn>      </mtd>      <mtd>       <mi>a</mi>      </mtd>      <mtd>       <mn>1</mn>      </mtd>     </mtr>     <mtr>      <mtd>       <mn>4</mn>      </mtd>      <mtd>       <mi>b</mi>      </mtd>      <mtd>       <mn>3</mn>      </mtd>     </mtr>         </mtable></mrow>  </mfenced><mfenced close="]" open="[">   <mrow>    <mtable equalrows='true' equalcolumns='true'>     <mtr>      <mtd>       <mrow>        <mo>&#x2212;</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow>      </mtd>      <mtd>       <mrow>        <mo>&#x2212;</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow>      </mtd>      <mtd>       <mrow>        <mo>&#x2212;</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow>      </mtd>     </mtr>     <mtr>      <mtd>       <mn>1</mn>      </mtd>      <mtd>       <mi>a</mi>      </mtd>      <mtd>       <mn>1</mn>      </mtd>     </mtr>     <mtr>      <mtd>       <mn>4</mn>      </mtd>      <mtd>       <mi>b</mi>      </mtd>      <mtd>       <mn>3</mn>      </mtd>     </mtr>         </mtable></mrow>  </mfenced></mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>12</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>b</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>a</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>a</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>12</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>b</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>∴</mo><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mi>I</mi><mo>⇒</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>⇒</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn></math>

</details>

### Q11. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <em><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi></math></em> and <em><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi></math></em> are non-singular square matrix of same order <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>3</mn></math>, then which of the following options is correct?

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><msup><mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><msup><mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><msup><mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><msup><mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><msup><mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><msup><mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>  
<math><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><msup><mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>  
<math><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><msup><mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><msup><mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>  
<math><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><msup><mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>  
<math><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><msup><mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><msup><mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

### Q12. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></msub></math> are consecutive terms of an arithmetic progression with common difference <math><mn>3,</mn></math> then the value of <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><msubsup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msubsup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msubsup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>27</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>81</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>162</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mn>162</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Apply <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>R</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>&#8596;</mo><msub><mi>R</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>R</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>R</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>&#8596;</mo><msub><mi>R</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>R</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><msubsup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msubsup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>-</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msubsup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>-</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><msubsup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
Applying <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>C</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>&#8596;</mo><msub><mi>C</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>C</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><msubsup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>9</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>27</mn><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>27</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>162</mn></math>

</details>

### Q13. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>4</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math> are two matrices, then the value of the determinant<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>A</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi>B</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>A</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>A</mi><mn>4</mn></msup><msup><mi>B</mi><mn>4</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mn>20 </mn><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi>ms</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>20</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>20</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>20</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mn>0</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>A</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>4</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>4</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>4</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>A</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>B</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi></math>
<math><msup><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>20</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi></math>
<math><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>20</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mi>A</mi></math>
So, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>A</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi>B</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>A</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>A</mi><mn>20</mn></msup><msup><mi>B</mi><mn>20</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>+</mo><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>20</mn><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mn>20</mn></mfenced><mn>3</mn></msup><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>A</mi></mfenced></math> &hellip;.(1)
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>4</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
Hence, from equation (1),
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>A</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi>B</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>A</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>A</mi><mn>20</mn></msup><msup><mi>B</mi><mn>20</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

</details>

### Q14. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi></math> are non-singular matrices of order three such that <math><mi>a</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>j</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>&#945;</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>&#945;</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>a</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>j</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></math>, then the value of <math><mi>&#945;</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>3</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>j</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>&#945;</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>&#945;</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math> and <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>a</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>j</mi><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mi>&#945;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>&#945;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn><mspace linebreak="newline"/><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>9</mn><mn>5</mn></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q15. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi></math> are square matrices of order <math><mn>3</mn></math>. If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>A</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>B</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>I</mi></math> and <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>j</mi><mi></mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>I</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>k</mi></math>, then the value of <math><mi>k</mi></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **2.25**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>I</mi></math>
Multiplying by <math><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
<math><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>I</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>…</mo><mo>…</mo><mo>.</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>j</mi><mi></mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>I</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>I</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2.25</mn></math>

</details>

mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mfenced mathvariant="normal">λ</mi><mo>≠</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math> then <math><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></math> is equal to

- **(A)** <math><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>&#8594;</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>&#8594;</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced></mtd><mtd><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced></mtd><mtd><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>&#8594;</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>&#8594;</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mtd><mtd><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mtd><mtd><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mtd><mtd><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mtd><mtd><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></math>

</details>
