### Q17. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mi>A</mi></math> is an invertible square matrix of the order <math><mi>n</mi></math> such that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mi>A</mi></mfenced><mo>≠</mo><mn>1</mn></math> and <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>j</mi><mi></mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>j</mi><mi></mi><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mi>n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></msup></math>, then the sum of all possible values of <math><mi>n</mi></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>3</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>6</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>5</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mn>5</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>We know that <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>j</mi><mi></mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>j</mi><mi></mi><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></msup></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><msup><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mi>A</mi></mfenced><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mi>A</mi></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>n</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mi>n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfenced></msup></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><msup><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mi>n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><msup><mi>n</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>Hence, the sum of all values of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>n</mi></math> is equal to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q18. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi></math> are non-singular matrices of order <math><mn>3</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo></math> such that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>adj</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>adj</mi><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> , then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>det</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>A</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>det</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>B</mi></mfenced></math> is equal to (where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>det</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>M</mi><mo>)</mo></math> represents the determinant of matrix <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>M</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>adj</mi><mi>M</mi></math> represents the adjoint matrix of matrix <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>M</mi></math>)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>3</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>2</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>adj</mi><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>adj</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>adj</mi><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>A</mi></mfenced><mi>A</mi></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
Hence, <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

</details>

---

### Q19. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mi>A</mi></math> and <math><mi>B</mi></math> are square matrices of order <math><mn>3</mn></math> such that <math><mi>A</mi><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>B</mi></math> and <math><mn>2</mn><mi>A</mi><msup><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mi>B</mi></math>, then the value of <math><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>16</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **4**

**Detailed Solution:**

Given, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><msup><mi>A</mi><mi>T</mi></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>B</mi></math>
Taking determinant on both sides, we get, 
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>27</mn><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> &hellip; <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
Also given, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>A</mi><msup><mi>B</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mi>A</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mi>B</mi></math>
Taking determinant on both sides, we get, 
<math><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#8901;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math> &hellip; <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
From <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>, we get, 
<math><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mn>27</mn><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn></math>

</details>

---

### Q20. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math> where <math><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>∈</mo><mi>R</mi></math>. If the sum of all non-real roots of the equation <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>k</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>m</mi><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mo>∀</mo><mi>k</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>m</mi><mo>∈</mo><mi>Z</mi></math>, then the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>k</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>m</mi></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **3**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>n</mi><mi>I</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>n</mi></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>n</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>b</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>n</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>n</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>n</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>n</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi><mi>w</mi><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi><msup><mrow><mi>w</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
Sum of non real roots <math><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>w</mi><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>w</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>k</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>m</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>k</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>m</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>

</details>

---

### Q21. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

For a singular matrix <math><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>i</mi><mi>j</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> and the matrix  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="["><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> if <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>B</mi><mi>A</mi><mo>,</mo></math> then <math><mn>3</mn><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>11</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **12**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>11</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow></msub></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>21</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>22</mn></mrow></msub></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>B</mi><mi>A</mi></math> (given)
i.e. <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mtable><mtr><mtd><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>11</mn></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>12</mn></msub></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>21</mn></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>22</mn></msub></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mtable><mtr><mtd><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>11</mn></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>12</mn></msub></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>21</mn></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>22</mn></msub></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>11</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>12</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>11</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>a</mi><mrow><mn>21</mn><mo>&#160;</mo></mrow></msub><mo>&#8230;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>11</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>12</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>12</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>a</mi><mrow><mn>22</mn><mo>&#160;</mo></mrow></msub><mo>&#8230;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>21</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>22</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>11</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>21</mn></msub><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8230;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>21</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>22</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>12</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>22</mn></msub><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8230;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
From <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>11</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>22</mn></mrow></msub></math>
From <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>21</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow></msub></math>
Since, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>A</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>11</mn></msub><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>22</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>21</mn></msub><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>12</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mfenced separators="|"><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>11</mn></msub></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mfenced separators="|"><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>12</mn></msub></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mspace linebreak="newline"/><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>11</mn></msub><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>12</mn></msub></mfrac></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math></p><p>Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>11</mn></msub><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>12</mn></msub></mfrac></mfenced><mn>4</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>12</mn></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q22. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi></math> are square matrices of order <math><mn>3</mn></math> such that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>A</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>B</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>, then the value of <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mi>a</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>j</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><msup><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mi>a</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>j</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>27</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>27</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>108</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>27</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>a</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>j</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><msup><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mi>a</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>j</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>j</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><msup><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>j</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo></math> <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
<math><mo>=</mo></math> <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>×</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>×</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>=</mo></math> <math><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>27</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mfenced mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mtd><mtd><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mtd><mtd><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi><mo>≠</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math> then <math><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></math> is equal to:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>→</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>→</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi></mrow></mfenced></mtd><mtd><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi></mrow></mfenced></mtd><mtd><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi></mrow></mfenced></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi></mrow></mfenced></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>→</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>→</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mtd><mtd><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mtd><mtd><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mtd><mtd><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mtd><mtd><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>∴</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

</details>

### Q24. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

<p>If the maximum and minimum values of the determinant

<math> <mrow> <mfenced close="|" open="|" separators=","> <mrow> <mtable> <mtr> <mtd> <mn>1</mn> <mo>+</mo> <msup> <mtext>sin</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>x</mo> </mtd> <mtd> <msup> <mtext>cos</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>x</mo> </mtd> <mtd> <mtext>sin 2x</mtext> </mtd> </mtr> <mtr> <mtd> <msup> <mtext>sin</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>x</mo> </mtd> <mtd> <mn>1</mn> <mo>+</mo> <msup> <mtext>cos</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mtext>x</mtext> </mtd> <mtd> <mtext>sin 2x</mtext> </mtd> </mtr> <mtr> <mtd> <msup> <mtext>sin</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mtext>x</mtext> </mtd> <mtd> <msup> <mtext>cos</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mtext>x</mtext> </mtd> <mtd> <mn>1</mn> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>sin 2x</mtext> </mtd> </mtr> </mtable> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>  are <math> <mrow> <mi>α</mi> </mrow> </math> and <math> <mrow> <mi>β</mi> </mrow> </math> respectively, then <math> <mrow> <mi>α</mi> <mo>+</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mi>β</mi> </mrow> </math> is equal to</p>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **5**

**Detailed Solution:**

Given determinant is 

<math> <mrow> <mrow> <mo>&#916;</mo> <mo>=</mo> </mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators=","> <mrow> <mtable> <mtr> <mtd> <mn>1</mn> <mo>+</mo> <msup> <mtext>sin</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>x</mo> </mtd> <mtd> <msup> <mtext>cos</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>x</mo> </mtd> <mtd> <mtext>sin 2x</mtext> </mtd> </mtr> <mtr> <mtd> <msup> <mtext>sin</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>x</mo> </mtd> <mtd> <mn>1</mn> <mo>+</mo> <msup> <mtext>cos</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mtext>x</mtext> </mtd> <mtd> <mtext>sin 2x</mtext> </mtd> </mtr> <mtr> <mtd> <msup> <mtext>sin</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mtext>x</mtext> </mtd> <mtd> <msup> <mtext>cos</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mtext>x</mtext> </mtd> <mtd> <mn>1</mn> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>sin 2x</mtext> </mtd> </mtr> </mtable> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>

Applying the operation <math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>C</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msub> <mo>&#8594;</mo> <msub> <mtext>C</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msub> <mo>+</mo> <msub> <mtext>C</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow> </math>, we get

<math> <mrow> <mo>&#916;</mo> <mo>=</mo> <mfenced close="|" open="|" separators=","> <mrow> <mtable> <mtr> <mtd> <mn>2</mn> </mtd> <mtd> <msup> <mtext>cos</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>x</mo> </mtd> <mtd> <mtext>sin 2x</mtext> </mtd> </mtr> <mtr> <mtd> <mn>2</mn> </mtd> <mtd> <mn>1</mn> <mo>+</mo> <msup> <mtext>cos</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>x</mo> </mtd> <mtd> <mtext>sin 2x</mtext> </mtd> </mtr> <mtr> <mtd> <mn>1</mn> </mtd> <mtd> <msup> <mtext>cos</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>x</mo> </mtd> <mtd> <mn>1</mn> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>sin 2x</mtext> </mtd> </mtr> </mtable> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>

Applying the operations  <math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>R</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mo>&#8594;</mo> <msub> <mtext>R</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mo>-</mo> <msub> <mtext>R</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msub> </mrow> </math>  and then <math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>R</mtext> <mn>3</mn> </msub> <mo>&#8594;</mo> <msub> <mtext>R</mtext> <mn>3</mn> </msub> <mo>-</mo> <msub> <mtext>R</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msub> </mrow> </math>, we get

<math> <mrow> <mo>&#916;</mo> <mo>=</mo> <mfenced close="|" open="|" separators=","> <mrow> <mtable> <mtr> <mtd> <mn>2</mn> </mtd> <mtd> <msup> <mtext>cos</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>x</mo> </mtd> <mtd> <mtext>sin 2x</mtext> </mtd> </mtr> <mtr> <mtd> <mn>0</mn> </mtd> <mtd> <mn>1</mn> </mtd> <mtd> <mn>0</mn> </mtd> </mtr> <mtr> <mtd> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mtd> <mtd> <mn>0</mn> </mtd> <mtd> <mn>1</mn> </mtd> </mtr> </mtable> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>

Now, expanding the above determinant along <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>R</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math>, we get

<math> <mrow> <mo>&#916;</mo> <mo>=</mo> <mo>-</mo> <mn>0</mn> <mo>+</mo> <mo>(</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>sin 2x</mtext> <mo>)</mo> <mo>-</mo> <mn>0</mn> <mo>=</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>sin 2x</mtext> </mrow> </math>

Since, the maximum value of <math> <mrow> <mtext>sin</mtext> <mtext>2x</mtext> </mrow> </math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn></math> and minimum value of <math> <mrow> <mtext>sin</mtext> <mtext>2x</mtext> </mrow> </math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>.

Therefore, <math> <mrow> <mi>&#945;</mi> <mo>=</mo> </mrow> </math>maximum value of <math> <mrow> <mo>&#916;</mo> <mo>=</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mo>+</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mo>=</mo> <mn>3</mn> </mrow> </math>  and  <math> <mrow> <mi>&#946;</mi> <mo>=</mo> </mrow> </math> minimum value of <math> <mrow> <mo>&#916;</mo> <mo>=</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mo>=</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </math>.

<math> <mrow> <mo>&#8658;&#160;&#160;</mo> <mi>&#945;</mi> <mo>=</mo> <mn>3</mn> </mrow> </math>  and <math> <mrow> <mi>&#946;</mi> <mo>=</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>&#8756;&#160;&#160;</mo> <mi>&#945;</mi> <mo>+</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mi>&#946;</mi> <mo>=</mo> <mn>3</mn> <mo>+</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mo>=</mo> <mn>5</mn> </mrow> </math>

</details>

---

### Q25. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mi>M</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mi>a</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>b</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>c</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>d</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>e</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>f</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mi>N</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>.</mo></math> If <math><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>d</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>e</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>36</mn><mo>,</mo></math> <math><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>e</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>f</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>64</mn><mo>,</mo></math> <math><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>d</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>f</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>100</mn></math>, then value of <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>N</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>1152</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>48</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>144</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>288</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mn>288</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mi>M</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo></math>Area of the triangle with vertices <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>d</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>b</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>e</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo> </mo><mo>&amp;</mo><mo> </mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>c</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>f</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> with the length of sides as <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>6</mn><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mn>8</mn><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mn>10</mn></math>
<math><mo>∵</mo></math> Triangle is a right angle triangle, hence, 
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Area</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>×</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>29</mn></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>M</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>48</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mi>N</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mfrac><msup><mi>M</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>8</mn></mfrac><msup><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mi>M</mi></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mn>48</mn></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mn>8</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>288</mn></math>

</details>

---

### Q26. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>, <math><msub><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> be a matrix formed by the cofactors of the elements of the matrix <math><mi>A</mi></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> be a matrix formed by the cofactors of the elements of matrix <math><msub><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>.</mo></math> Similarly, if <math><msub><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></msub></math> be a matrix formed by the cofactors of the elements of matrix <math><msub><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></msub></math>, then the value of <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(B)** <math><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>20</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>9</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1024</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1024</mn></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><msub><mi>A</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>A</mi></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup></math>
Similarly, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><msub><mi>A</mi><mn>10</mn></msub></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>A</mi></mfenced><msup><mn>2</mn><mn>10</mn></msup></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mn>3</mn><msup><mn>2</mn><mn>10</mn></msup></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mn>3</mn><mrow><mn>1</mn><mn>024</mn></mrow></msup></math>

</details>

### Q27. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>≠</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>B</mi><mi>A</mi></math> and <math><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo></math> then the value of the determinant of matrix <math><mi>A</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>B</mi></math> is (where <math><mi>A</mi></math> and <math><mi>B</mi></math> are square matrices of order <math><mn>3</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>3</mn></math> )

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mn>0</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>given</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>B</mi><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
Since, <math><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8800;</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>B</mi></math> is not a null matrix
Hence, <math><mi>A</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>B</mi></math> is a null matrix or <math><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
In both cases <math><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

</details>

### Q28. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>6</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>8</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>12</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd><mtd><mn>16</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>α</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mn>0</mn></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo></math> If the sum of all possible values of <math><mi>α</mi></math> is <math><mi>k</mi><mi>π</mi></math> for <math><mi>α</mi><mo>∈</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>0,2</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> then the value of <math><mi>k</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>6</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>8</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mn>6</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>6</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>8</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>12</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd><mtd><mn>16</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>α</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>6</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
Let, <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
(<math><mo>∵</mo></math> both values are negative so the solution lies in <math><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi></mrow></msup></math> or <math><msup><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi></mrow></msup></math> quadrant)
<math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mi>α</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>π</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>π</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi></math>
Hence, sum of all possible values<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>π</mi></math>

</details>

### Q29. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>A</mi><mi>r</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mi>r</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>r</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msup><mn>3</mn><mi>r</mi></msup></mfrac></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> then <math><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo>→</mo><mi>∞</mi></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">∑</mo><mrow><mi>r</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></munderover><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>8</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>2</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>r</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>r</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>r</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mtd><mtd><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>&#8734;</mi></mrow></munder><mo>&#8289;</mo><munderover><mo>&#8721;</mo><mrow><mi>r</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mi>n</mi></munderover><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><msub><mi>A</mi><mi>r</mi></msub></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo></mtd><mtd><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msup><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msup><mn>3</mn><mn>3</mn></msup></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mo>&#8230;</mo></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfrac><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfrac></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q30. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math> be a scalar matrix whose elements are the roots of the equation <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>9</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>15</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>8</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>75</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>7</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>125</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>6</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>. If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>&#183;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>k</mi></math>, then the value of <math><mi>k</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msup><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(B)** <math><msup><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><msup><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>9</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>15</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>8</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>75</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>7</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>125</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>6</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mspace linebreak="newline"></mspace><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>6</mn></msup><msup><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>)</mo></mrow><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0,0</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>0,0</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>0,0</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>5,5</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>5</mn></math>
So, <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>5</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>5</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>5</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>&#183;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mfenced><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mfenced separators="|"><msup><mn>5</mn><mn>3</mn></msup></mfenced><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mn>5</mn><mn>9</mn></msup></math>

</details>

### Q31. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>i</mi><mi>j</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></msub></math> is a matrix such that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>a</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mi>j</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close="" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>&#8704;</mo><mi>i</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>j</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>&#8704;</mo><mi>i</mi><mo>&#8800;</mo><mi>j</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>. ### Q33. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="["><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>5</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="["><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>. If <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>, where <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi></math> is a square matrix of order <math><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo></math> then the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Tr</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to (where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Tr</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>)</mo></math> represents the trace of a matrix <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> i.e. the sum of all the diagonal elements of the matrix <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>adjB</mi></math> represents the adjoint matrix of matrix <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math>)

**Options:**

- **(B)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Given, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>PQ</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi></mfrac></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow><mn>10</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>
<math><mi>Z</mi><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mo>.</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mo>.</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Tr</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>.</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Tr</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mo>.</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>Tr</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p>

### Q34. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>4</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>q</mi><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>r</mi><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>s</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math> be an identity, where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>r</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>s</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>t</mi></math> are constants, then the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>s</mi></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **71**

**Detailed Solution:**

Differentiating both sides, we get,
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><mi>p</mi><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>q</mi><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mi>r</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>s</mi></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
Putting <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>, we get, <math><mi>s</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mi>s</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>64</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>7</mn><mn>1</mn></math>

</details>
