### Q35. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>λ</mi><mo>∈</mo><mi>N</mi><mo>,</mo></math> then the least possible value of <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>λ</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>a</mi><mi>c</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>b</mi><mi>a</mi></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>λ</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>c</mi><mi>a</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>c</mi><mi>b</mi></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>λ</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math> is

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **4**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Multiplying first, second, third rows by <math><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>b</mi><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>c</mi></math> and then taking <math><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>b</mi><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>c</mi></math> common from first, second, third columns, we get,
<math><mi>D</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>Now, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>R</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>&#8596;</mo><msub><mi>R</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>R</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>R</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>Now, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>C</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>&#8596;</mo><msub><mi>C</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>C</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>C</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>&#8594;</mo><msub><mi>C</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>C</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><mi>&#955;</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>&#955;</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>&#955;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
Hence, <math><msub><mrow><mi>D</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math> when <math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q36. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mi>A</mi></math> and <math><mi>B</mi></math> are square matrices such that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>A</mi><mn>2020</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mi>O</mi></math> and <math><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mi></mi><mo>=</mo><mi></mi><mi>A</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>B,</mi></math> then <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to (where, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>O</mi></math> is a null matrix)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mn>0</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>A</mi><mn>2020</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mi>O</mi><mo>⇒</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mi>A</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>A</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>I</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
Taking determinant on both sides, we get,
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>I</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>⇒</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> (as <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>)

</details>

---

### Q37. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#946;</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#947;</mi></math> are the roots of the equation <math><mi>p</mi><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>q</mi><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>r</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math> then the value of the determinant <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>&#945;</mi><mi>&#946;</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>&#946;</mi><mi>&#947;</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>&#947;</mi><mi>&#945;</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>&#946;</mi><mi>&#947;</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>&#947;</mi><mi>&#945;</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>&#945;</mi><mi>&#946;</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>&#947;</mi><mi>&#945;</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>&#945;</mi><mi>&#946;</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>&#946;</mi><mi>&#947;</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi>p</mi><mi>q</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi>q</mi><mi>r</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi>p</mi><mi>r</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mn>0</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>The roots of the equation <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mtext mathvariant="italic">px</mtext><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mtext mathvariant="italic">qx</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mtext mathvariant="italic">r</mtext><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> are <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#947;</mi></math>
i.e. <math><mi>&#945;</mi><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mi>&#947;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#947;</mi><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>&#945;</mi><mi>&#946;</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>&#946;</mi><mi>&#947;</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>&#947;</mi><mi>&#945;</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>&#946;</mi><mi>&#947;</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>&#947;</mi><mi>&#945;</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>&#945;</mi><mi>&#946;</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>&#947;</mi><mi>&#945;</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>&#945;</mi><mi>&#946;</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>&#946;</mi><mi>&#947;</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math></p><p>Applying <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mtext>C</mtext><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>&#8594;</mo><msub><mtext>C</mtext><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mtext>C</mtext><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mtext>C</mtext><mn>3</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>&#945;</mi><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mi>&#947;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#947;</mi><mi>&#945;</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>&#946;</mi><mi>&#947;</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>&#947;</mi><mi>&#945;</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>&#945;</mi><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mi>&#947;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#947;</mi><mi>&#945;</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>&#947;</mi><mi>&#945;</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>&#945;</mi><mi>&#946;</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>&#945;</mi><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mi>&#947;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#947;</mi><mi>&#945;</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>&#945;</mi><mi>&#946;</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>&#946;</mi><mi>&#947;</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q38. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mi>A</mi></math> be a non-singular matrix of order <math><mn>3</mn></math> such that <math><mi>A</mi><mi></mi><mi>a</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>j</mi><mi></mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>A</mi><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow></msup></math>, then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mroot><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><msup><mi>A</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mfenced><mn>3</mn></mroot></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **1.8**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>j</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>A</mi><msup><mi>A</mi><mi>T</mi></msup></math>
Taking determinant on both sides, we get,
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>j</mi><mi></mi><mn>3</mn><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>A</mi><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mn>3</mn><mn>6</mn></msup><msup><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>A</mi></mfenced><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mn>5</mn><mn>3</mn></msup><msup><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>A</mi></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mspace linebreak="newline"/><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>A</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfrac><mn>125</mn><mn>729</mn></mfrac><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>A</mi></mfenced><mo>&#8800;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
Hence, <math><mroot><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mroot><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfrac bevelled="true"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1.8</mn></math>

</details>

---

### Q39. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>   2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>   0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>   3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>   3</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>   1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math> be a matrix, then <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>   2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>   0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>   3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>   3</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>   1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>&nbsp&nbsp&nbsp1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>&nbsp&nbsp&nbsp3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>&nbsp&nbsp&nbsp0</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>&nbsp&nbsp&nbsp2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>   2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>   0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>   3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>   3</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>   1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

We know that, 

<math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math>

<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>.</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math>

<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math>

<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math>

</details>

---

### Q40. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mn>3</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><munderover><mo>&#8721;</mo><mrow><mi>r</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>5</mn></munderover><msub><mi>D</mi><mi>r</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><munderover><mo>&#8721;</mo><mrow><mi>r</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>5</mn></munderover><mi>r</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>15</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>8</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><munderover><mo>&#8721;</mo><mrow><mi>r</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>5</mn></munderover><msup><mi>r</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mtd><mtd><mn>35</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>9</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><munderover><mo>&#8721;</mo><mrow><mi>r</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>5</mn></munderover><msup><mi>r</mi><mn>3</mn></msup></mtd><mtd><mn>25</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>10</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>15</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>15</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>8</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>55</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>35</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>9</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>225</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>25</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>10</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
applying <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>C</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>&#8596;</mo><msub><mi>C</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>C</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math>, we get,
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><munderover><mo>&#8721;</mo><mrow><mi>r</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>5</mn></munderover><msub><mi>D</mi><mi>r</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>15</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>8</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>20</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>35</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>9</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>200</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>25</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>10</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2800</mn><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mroot><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>100</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">&#8721;</mo><mrow><mi>r</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></munderover><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>D</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mn>37</mn></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mroot><mo>=</mo><mroot><mrow><mn>243</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mroot><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>

</details>

### Q44. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi></math> is a square matrix of order <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>.</mo></math> The matrices <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>4</mn><msup><mi>A</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi>A</mi><mi>T</mi></msup></math>all have the same value of the determinant. If <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>j</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow></msup></math>, then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><msup><mi>A</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>27</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>3</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>27</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>64</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>64</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn></math> or <math><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8757;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>j</mi><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>A</mi><mi>T</mi></msup><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>j</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>A</mi></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>A</mi><mi>T</mi></msup></mrow></mfenced><mspace linebreak="newline"/><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>A</mi></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>A</mi></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>A</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0,8</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn></math>
So, <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>27</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>27</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q45. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

For a matrix <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi></math>, <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>i</mi><mi>j</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>&#8800;</mo><mn>0</mn></math> . If each element <math><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>i</mi><mi>j</mi></mrow></msub></math> is multiplied by <math><msup><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>j</mi></mrow></msup></math>, we obtain matrix <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi></math>, such that <math><mi>&#945;</mi><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0,</mn></math> then the value of <math><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **0**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Let, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>11</mn></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>12</mn></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>13</mn></msub></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>21</mn></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>22</mn></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>23</mn></msub></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>31</mn></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>32</mn></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>33</mn></msub></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> then</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>11</mn></msub></mtd><mtd><mfrac><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>12</mn></msub><mi>k</mi></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mfrac><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>13</mn></msub><msup><mi>k</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>k</mi><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>21</mn></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>22</mn></msub></mtd><mtd><mfrac><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>23</mn></msub><mi>k</mi></mfrac></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mi>k</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>31</mn></msub></mtd><mtd><mi>k</mi><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>32</mn></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>33</mn></msub></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>B</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>11</mn></msub></mtd><mtd><mfrac><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>12</mn></msub><mi>k</mi></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mfrac><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>13</mn></msub><msup><mi>k</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>k</mi><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>21</mn></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>22</mn></msub></mtd><mtd><mfrac><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>23</mn></msub><mi>k</mi></mfrac></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mi>k</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>31</mn></msub></mtd><mtd><mi>k</mi><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>32</mn></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>33</mn></msub></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>11</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><mi>k</mi><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>13</mn></mrow></msub></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>21</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><mi>k</mi><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>22</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>23</mn></mrow></msub></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>31</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>k</mi><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>32</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>33</mn></mrow></msub></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>11</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>13</mn></mrow></msub></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>21</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>22</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>23</mn></mrow></msub></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>31</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>32</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>33</mn></mrow></msub></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>-</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
On comparison, <math><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q46. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd><mtd><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd><mtd><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math> and matrix <math><mi>B</mi></math> is defined such that <math><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup></math> . If <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn></math> , then the number of values of <math><mi>α</mi></math> in <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mtext> </mtext><mn>10</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>10</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>12</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>5</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>3</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mn>10</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>A</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>I</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>A</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>A</mi><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>I</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mtext>&#8201;&#8201;</mtext><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>I</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>&#8230;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>I</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd><mtd><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>&#945;</mi><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
From <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mfrac><mrow><mn>11</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mfrac><mrow><mn>13</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>15</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>17</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mfrac><mrow><mn>19</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
Total number of values <math><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn></math>

</details>

### Q47. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> are the angles of a triangle and <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd><mtd><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd><mtd><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd></mtr></mtable><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>0,</mn></math> then triangle <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** right angled isosceles
- **(B)** isosceles
- **(C)** equilateral
- **(D)** scalene

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) isosceles**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>→</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math>

<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd><mtd><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd><mtd><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd><mtd><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd><mtd><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd></mtr></mtable><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>→</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd><mtd><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd><mtd><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd><mtd><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd><mtd><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd></mtr></mtable><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>×</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>×</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo></math> Either <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math> or <math><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> or <math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math>

So <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi></math> is isosceles

</details>

### Q48. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mi>A</mi></math> be a square matrix of order <math><mn>3</mn></math>, such that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mi>A</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>5</mn></msqrt></math>, then <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>j</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **29.16**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>j</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup><mo>×</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup><mo>×</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>25</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>729</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>25</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>29.16</mn></math>

</details>

### Q49. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let the matrix <math><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mi>B</mi><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>A</mi></math> where <math><mi>B</mi></math> represent <math><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>3</mn></math> order matrix. If the total number of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn></math> in matrix <math><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math> and matrix <math><mi>B</mi></math> are <math><mi>p</mi></math> and <math><mi>q</mi></math> respectively, then the value of <math><mi>p</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>q</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>3</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>5</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>7</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mn>7</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> and <math><mi>B</mi><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>A</mi></math>
<math><mi>B</mi><mi>A</mi><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mi>A</mi><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>I</mi></math>
Total number of <math><mn>1</mn></math> in matrix <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math> and matrix <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>A</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math></p><p>Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>7</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q50. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Consider a square matrix <math><mi>A</mi></math> of order <math><mn>2</mn></math> which has its elements as <math><mn>0,1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2,4</mn><mo>.</mo></math> If the absolute value of <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is least then, the absolute value of <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>j</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>j</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>2</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

For absolute least value of <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>4</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>or</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>4</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>&#177;</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>j</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>j</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>&#177;</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
So, absolute value is equal to <math><mn>2</mn></math>

</details>

### Q51. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>x</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mi>y</mi><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>b</mi></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>m</mi></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>c</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mi>y</mi><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>d</mi></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>n</mi></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>5</mn><mi>m</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn><mi>b</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn><mi>n</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>4</mn><mi>d</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>5</mn><mi>m</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn><mi>c</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn><mi>n</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn><mi>b</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn><mi>c</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>4</mn><mi>d</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> then the values of <math><mi>x</mi></math> and <math><mi>y</mi></math> are

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Taking log in both the equations, we get, 
<math><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>b</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>m</mi></math>
<math><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>d</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>n</mi></math>
By Cramer&rsquo;s Rule, 
<math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></math>
<math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></math>

</details>

---

### Q52. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>∈</mo><mi>N</mi></math> and <math><mi>D</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mi>a</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>b</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>c</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>c</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>a</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>b</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>b</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>c</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>a</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>, then the least positive value of <math><mi>D</mi></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>6</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>3</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>8</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mn>4</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mi>a</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>b</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>c</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>c</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>a</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>b</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>b</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>c</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>a</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>c</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
For least positive value, when <math><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>∈</mo><mi>N,</mi></math> two of them are <math><mn>1</mn></math> and one of them is <math><mn>2</mn></math>
So, <math><msub><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>D</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>

</details>

---

### Q53. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1,2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2,8</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> are ordered triplet pair of the form <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> which satisfy all the equations <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mi>a</mi></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mi>y</mi><mi>b</mi></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mi>z</mi><mi>c</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mi>b</mi></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mi>y</mi><mi>c</mi></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mi>z</mi><mi>a</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mi>c</mi></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mi>y</mi><mi>a</mi></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mi>z</mi><mi>b</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo></math> then the sum of all the values of <math><mi>&#945;</mi></math> is equal to (where, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8800;</mo></math><math><mn>0</mn></math>)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>3</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>2</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

System of equation has more than one solution
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8710;</mo><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>a</mi></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>b</mi></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>c</mi></mfrac></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>b</mi></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>c</mi></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>a</mi></mfrac></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>c</mi></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>a</mi></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>b</mi></mfrac></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mspace linebreak="newline"/><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>a</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>b</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>c</mi></mfrac><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>c</mi></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo></math> all three equations are <math><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi></math>
Now, <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2,8</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> satisfy this equation
<math><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>
<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1,2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> also satisfy this equation
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> also satisfy this equation
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
Sum of the values of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi></math> <math><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

</details>

---

### Q54. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mi>A</mi></math> be a square matrix of order <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn></math> such that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>A</mi><mi>T</mi></msup><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>10</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>6</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>21</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>12</mn></msub></mtd><mtd><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>6</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>23</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>32</mn></msub></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>31</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>13</mn></msub></mtd><mtd><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>8</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> where <math><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>23</mn></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>31</mn></mrow></msub></math> are positive roots of the equation <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>p</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8704;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>R</mi><mo>,</mo></math> then the absolute value of <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **37**

**Detailed Solution:**

Clearly, <math><mi>A</mi><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow></msup></math> is a symmetric matrix
Also, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>12</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>23</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>31</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>12</mn></msub><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>23</mn></msub><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>31</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn></math>
 <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mi>M</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>G</mi><mi>M</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>12</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>23</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>31</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>21</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>21</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>31</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>13</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>31</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>13</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>23</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>32</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>23</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>32</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>
For diagonal elements
<math><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>11</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>11</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math>
<math><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>22</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>22</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>
<math><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>33</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>33</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>5</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>4</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>6</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>16</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>12</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>8</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>50</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>16</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mn>7</mn></math>

</details>

### Q55. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi>s </mi><mi>θ</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi>s </mi><mn>2</mn><mi>θ</mi></mtd><mtd><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mn>7</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>7</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>, then the number of values of <math><mi>θ</mi></math> in <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>0,2</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>3</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>2</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>7</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>7</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>⇒</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi></mi><mi>θ</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi></mi><mi>θ</mi><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>⇒</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>±</mo><msqrt><mn>65</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msqrt><mn>65</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>65</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
Hence, <math><mn>2</mn></math> solutions are possible

</details>

### Q56. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mi>b</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>c</mi></math> are distinct positive real numbers such that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mi>a</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>b</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>c</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>b</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>c</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>a</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>c</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>a</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>b</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mtd><mtd><mi>a</mi><mi>c</mi><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mtd><mtd><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>c</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>a</mi><mi>c</mi><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mtd><mtd><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>c</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mtd><mtd><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>c</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mtd><mtd><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mtd><mtd><mi>a</mi><mi>c</mi><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>, then

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>
- **(B)** <math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>
- **(D)** <math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Elements of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> are co-factors of the elements of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> 
Hence <math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>

</details>
