### Q57. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mi>B</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>C</mi></math> are square matrices of order <math><mn>3</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mi>A</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mi>B</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mi>C</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo></math> then the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>j</mi><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>B</mi><msup><mi>C</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to (where, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>j</mi><mi>A</mi></math> represents the adjoint matrix of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi></math>)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>27</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>27</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>81</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>81</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mn>81</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>j</mi><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>B</mi><msup><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>j</mi><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>⋅</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>⋅</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>×</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn><mn>1</mn></math>

</details>

### Q58. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> and <math><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math> are two matrices of order <math><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>3</mn></math>, where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>, then <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>8</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

We know that,
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mi></mi><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math> (for <math><mn>3</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>3</mn></math> matrices)
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>×</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup><mo>×</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

</details>

### Q59. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math> be the adjoint matrix of matrix <math><mi>B</mi></math> such that <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn></math>, then the value of <math><mi>&#955;</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>77</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>23</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>39</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>77</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>j</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>λ</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>6</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>λ</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi>λ</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>77</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q60. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><munderover><mo>&#8721;</mo><mrow><mi>r</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>10</mn></munderover><msup><mi>A</mi><mi>r</mi></msup></math>, then the value of det <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **0**

**Detailed Solution:**

Note <math><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Ι</mi><mo>⇒</mo><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Ι</mi></math> (if <math><mi>n</mi></math> is even) &amp; <math><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mi>A</mi></math> (if <math><mi>n</mi></math> is odd)
So, <math><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Ι</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>⇒</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

</details>

### Q61. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>4</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>j</mi><mi>A</mi></math> and <math><mi>C</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>A</mi><mo>,</mo></math> then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>j</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mi>C</mi></mfenced></mfrac></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>8</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>16</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>72</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mn>8</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>4</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>9</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>13</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>j</mi><mi></mi><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>j</mi><mi></mi><mi>a</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>j</mi><mi></mi><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>36</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>C</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mn>3</mn><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>6</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>j</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>C</mi></mfenced></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>36</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>36</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mn>3</mn><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn></math>

</details>

### Q62. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mi>y</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>z</mi></math> are the roots of the equation <math><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow></mfenced><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>111</mn><mi>t</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2020</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math> then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>y</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math> is equal to (where, <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> denotes the greatest integral value less than or equal to <math><mi>x</mi></math>)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>20</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mo>–</mo><mn>10</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mn>0</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is an integer</p><p><math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mrow></math>
Hence, the sum of roots <math><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>y</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>z</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q63. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math> be a matrix and <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup></math>, then the sum of all the values of <math><mi>x</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>24</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>24</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>18</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mo>-</mo><mn>18</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup></math> (By property)
<math><mo>⇒</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mo>⇒</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>±</mo><mn>12</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>=</mo><mo>±</mo><mn>12</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>21</mn></math>

</details>

### Q64. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>a</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>b</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>5</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>c</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> where <math><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>b</mi><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>c</mi></math> are positive integers. If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>t</mi><mi>r</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>A</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>7</mn></math>, then the greatest value of <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is (where <math><mi>t</mi><mi>r</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> denotes the trace of matrix <math><mi>A</mi></math> i.e. the sum of principal diagonal elements of matrix <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi></math>)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>6</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>12</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>15</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>10</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mn>15</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi>t</mi><mi>r</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>7</mn></math>
Ordered triplets of <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> are <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1,1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1,2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1,3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,1</mo><mo>,</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,2</mo><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,3</mo><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,4</mo><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>,1</mo><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>,2</mo><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>,3</mo><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>,1</mo><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>,2</mo><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>5</mn><mo>,1</mo><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mi>A</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>a</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>b</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>5</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>c</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mi>b</mi><mn>3</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is greatest for <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2,3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and equal to <math><mn>15</mn></math>

</details>

separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mi></mi><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mi></mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>&#947;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mi></mi><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mi></mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mi></mi><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>&#947;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math> {where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>&#945;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi>&#946;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>&#947;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></math> are all distinct}, then the value of <math><mi>n</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>3</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Given, <math><msup><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>n</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>n</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>&#947;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>n</mi></mrow></msup></math> <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>&#947;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>&#947;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi>&#947;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><msup><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi>&#947;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>)</mo><mi></mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi>&#947;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mi>&#945;</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>n</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mi>&#946;</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>n</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mi>&#947;</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>n</mi></mrow></msup><mo>[</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>&#945;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>&#946;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>&#946;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>&#947;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>&#947;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>&#945;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>]</mo><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>&#945;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>&#946;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>&#946;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>&#947;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced><msup><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi>&#947;</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>&#945;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>)</mo></mrow><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mi>&#946;</mi><mi>&#947;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>4</mn></msup></mfrac></math>
On comparison, we get,
<math><mi>n</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

</details>

---

### Q66. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<p>The value of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></math> for which the system of equations</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0,</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>14</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>11</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> has a non-trivial solution, is (here, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi></math>)</p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(D)** None of these

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

The system of equations has a non-trivial solution if and only if <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd><mtd><mn>8</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>14</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>11</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>0</mn></math>

Applying <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>→</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>4</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>→</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>7</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math>, we get,

<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>5</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>10</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>0</mn></math>

Expanding along <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>, we get,

<math><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>4</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>7</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mn>4</mn><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>4</mn><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>4</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> or <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mrow></math> .

[ <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mrow></math> is not possible]

<math><mo>∴</mo></math> For, <math><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></math> the system of equations has a non-trivial solution.

</details>

---

### Q67. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The number of values of <math><mi>x</mi></math> in <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>, for which the matrix <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math> is singular, is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>3</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>5</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mn>1</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>For the matrix to be singular, its determinant value should be zero

<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>6</mn><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi>x</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><mi>x</mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>5</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mspace linebreak="newline"></mspace><mo>⇒</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></math></p><p>But, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is rejected</p><p>Hence, only one value in <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="]" open="["><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q68. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>b</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>c</mi></math> satisfy the system of equations <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>12</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>6</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>. If the roots of the equation <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>a</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>b</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>c</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> are <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">&#945;</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">&#946;</mi></math>, then <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow></mfrac></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>243</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>100</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>243</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>100</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>243</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>100</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>243</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Using cramer’s rule
<math><mo>∆</mo><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>5</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>6</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>6</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>9</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>54</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>36</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>54</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>72</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>18</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>36</mn></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mo>∆</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>6</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>12</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>5</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>6</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>9</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>54</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>24</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>88</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>324</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>48</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>264</mn><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>12</mn></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mo>∆</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>6</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>12</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>6</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>6</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>24</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>36</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>56</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>24</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>216</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>168</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>24</mn></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mo>∆</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>6</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>5</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>12</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>6</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>9</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>4</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>88</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>56</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>88</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>112</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>36</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>60</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mo>∆</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mo>∆</mo></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mo>∆</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mo>∆</mo></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mo>∆</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mo>∆</mo></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo></math> equation is <math><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>27</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mn>540</mn><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>100</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>243</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mi>α</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>β</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>27</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>α</mi><mi>β</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>20</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>β</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>α</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>β</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>α</mi><mi>β</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac bevelled="true"><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>27</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac bevelled="true"><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>20</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>100</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>243</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q69. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mn>2</mn><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></msup><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msup><mn>2</mn><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></msup><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><msup><mn>2</mn><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></msup><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msup><mn>2</mn><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></msub></msup></math> are in geometric progression, then <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(B)** <math><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(D)** None of these

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msup><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></math> are in <math><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi><mo>.</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo></math> are in <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>.</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></math> .

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo></math>

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math>

So, by operation <math><msub><mrow><mi>R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi> </mi><mo>→</mo><msub><mrow><mi>R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi> </mi><mo>(</mo><msub><mrow><mi>R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>)</mo></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

</details>

### Q70. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The number of positive integral solutions of the equation <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>y</mi></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>z</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi><msup><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>z</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi><msup><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi><msup><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>11</mn><mi> </mi></math> is/are

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>6</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>12</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Multiplying <math><msub><mrow><mi>R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> by <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi></math>, <math><msub><mrow><mi>R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> by <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi>R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math> by <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>z</mi></math> and dividing the determinant by <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mi>y</mi><mi>z</mi></math>, we get, 

<math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mi>y</mi><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mi>y</mi></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mi>z</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi><msup><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mi>z</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi><msup><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi><msup><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>11</mn></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mi>y</mi><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mi>y</mi><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>3</mn></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>3</mn></msup></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mi>z</mi><mn>3</mn></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mi>z</mi><mn>3</mn></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mi>z</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>11</mn></math>

Use <math><msub><mrow><mi>R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>→</mo><msub><mrow><mi>R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mi> </mi></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>z</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>3</mn></msup></mtd><mtd><mi> </mi><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>3</mn></msup></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mi>z</mi><mn>3</mn></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mi>z</mi><mn>3</mn></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mi>z</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>11</mn></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>z</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn></math> (as the value of the determinant is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn></math>)

Hence, all the possible solutions are <math><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo></math>

</details>

### Q71. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Let matrix <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>, where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext>x</mtext><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mtext>y</mtext><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mtext>z</mtext><mo>∈</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>. If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mrow><mi>adj</mi><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mrow><mi>adj</mi><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mrow><mi>adj</mi><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mi>adjA</mi></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mn>4</mn><mn>8</mn></msup><mo>·</mo><msup><mn>5</mn><mn>16</mn></msup></math>, then the number of such matrices <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> is equal to (where, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mi>M</mi></mfenced></math> represents determinant of a matrix <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>M</mi></math>)

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **36**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mo>.</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></msup></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>16</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mo>.</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>16</mn></mrow></msup></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>±</mo><mn>10</mn></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>±</mo><mn>10</mn></math>
<math><mo>∵</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>∈</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn></math> (not possible)
Hence, <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn></math>
The number of such matrices <math><msup><mrow><mo>=</mo><mi></mi></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></msup><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mn>6</mn></math>

</details>

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If the matrix <math xmlans="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="["><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>&#945;</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>&#945;</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>&#8704;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>R</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>&#62;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>A</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>144</mn><mo>,</mo></math> then the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mi>A</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>t</mi><mi>r</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>A</mi></mfenced></math> is equal to (where <math><msub><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> represent the trace of the matrix <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi></math> i.e. the sum of all the principal diagonal elements  of the matrix <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mi>A</mi></mfenced></math> is the determinant value of <math><mi>A</mi></math>)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>8</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>15</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>20</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>24</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>15</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>144</mn></math>
<math><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>I</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>144</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi></mi><msup><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>36</mn></math>
<math><mi>&#945;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn></math> or <math><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></math>
<math><msup><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> or <math><msup><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> or <math><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>&#177;</mo><msqrt><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>24</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math> or <math><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math> (rejected) or <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>&#177;</mo><mi>i</mi><mfrac><msqrt><mn>23</mn></msqrt><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math> (rejected)
<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>t</mi><mi>r</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>t</mi><mi>r</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>15</mn></math>

</details>

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mo>&#8710;</mo><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msub><mrow><mi xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mo>&#8710;</mo><mo>'</mo></msup><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi hence <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mn>25</mn></math>

### Q75. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>k</mi><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>y</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>, then <math><mi>k</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>3</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
Using property of determinant we can write this determinant as a sum of
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
Similarly,
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>y</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>y</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>y</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>y</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mi>y</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mi>y</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mi>y</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>y</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>y</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>y</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>y</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mi>k</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
