### Q76. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The value of the  determinant <math><mo>&#8710;</mo><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><msqrt><mn>13</mn></msqrt><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>5</mn></msqrt></mtd><mtd><msqrt><mn>5</mn></msqrt></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msqrt><mn>15</mn></msqrt><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>26</mn></msqrt></mtd><mtd><mn>5</mn></mtd><mtd><msqrt><mn>10</mn></msqrt></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>65</mn></msqrt></mtd><mtd><msqrt><mn>15</mn></msqrt></mtd><mtd><mn>5</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>15</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mo>-</mo><mn>25</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>25</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mo>-</mo><mn>15</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>3</mn><msqrt><mn>5</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(D)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>15</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mn>15</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mo>-</mo><mn>25</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Taking  <math><msqrt><mn>5</mn></msqrt></math>  common from  <math><msub><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> and  <math><msub><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math> , we get

 <math><mo>∆</mo><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><mn>5</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><msqrt><mn>13</mn></msqrt><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msqrt><mn>15</mn></msqrt><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>26</mn></msqrt></mtd><mtd><msqrt><mn>5</mn></msqrt></mtd><mtd><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>65</mn></msqrt></mtd><mtd><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mtd><mtd><msqrt><mn>5</mn></msqrt></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math> 

Applying  <math><msub><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>→</mo><msub><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msqrt><mn>13</mn></msqrt><msub><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><msub><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo></math>  we get

 <math><mo>∆</mo><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><msqrt><mn>5</mn></msqrt></mtd><mtd><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mtd><mtd><msqrt><mn>5</mn></msqrt></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>5</mn><mo>-</mo><msqrt><mn>6</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfenced></math> 

 <math><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><mn>18</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>25</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mn>15</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mo>-</mo><mn>25</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></math>

</details>

### Q77. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mi>b</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>c</mi></math> are the roots of the equation <math><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>13</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and <math><mi>A</mi></math> and <math><mi>B</mi></math> are two matrices given by <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>a</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>b</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>c</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>b</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>c</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>a</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>c</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>a</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>b</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mtd><mtd><mi>c</mi><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mtd><mtd><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>c</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>c</mi><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mtd><mtd><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>c</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mtd><mtd><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>c</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mtd><mtd><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mtd><mtd><mi>c</mi><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> then the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mi>A</mi></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mi>B</mi></mfenced></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>154</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>154</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>22</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>22</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>154</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>7</mn></math>
<math><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn></math>
<math><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>13</mn></math>
<math><mi>B</mi></math> is the cofactor matrix of <math><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mi>A</mi></mfenced><mo>&#8901;</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mi>B</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mi>A</mi></mfenced><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>.</mo><mfenced><mtext>i</mtext></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>A</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>a</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>b</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>c</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>b</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>c</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>a</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>c</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>a</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>b</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>c</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>c</mi><mn>3</mn></msup></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>49</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>27</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>154</mn></math>
From <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>154</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></math>

</details>

### Q78. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>B</mi></math> and <math><mi>C</mi></math> are <math><mi>n</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mi>n</mi></math> matrices such that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>A</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>B</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>C</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>.</mo></math> If <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>C</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1728</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>125</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>, then the value of <math><mi>n</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>3</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>6</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mn>2</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>C</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>C</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>n</mi></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mn>12</mn><mn>5</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mn>12</mn><mn>5</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>n</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

</details>

### Q79. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If one of the roots of the equation <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>7</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>6</mn></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>25</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>25</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>25</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>7</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3,</mn></math> then the sum of all other five roots is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Let the roots of expansion of the given determinant is <math><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>d</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>e</mi></math> and <math><mn>3</mn></math> (given)
<math><mo>&#8757;</mo><mi></mi></math> the given determinant represents a <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>6</mn></math>degree polynomial and contains even power of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo></math>coefficient of <math><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo></math> the sum of other roots <math><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo></math> the sum of other roots <math><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q80. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Let <em>A</em> be a matrix of order 3 × 3 such that det(<em>A</em>) = 2, <em>B</em> = 2<em>A</em><sup>–1</sup> and <math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mroot><mrow><mn>16</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mroot></mrow></mfrac></math> , then the value of det(<em>A</em><sup>3</sup><em>B</em><sup>2</sup><em>C</em><sup>3</sup>) is

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **2**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msup><mn>2</mn><mn>3</mn></msup><mrow><mo>|</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>|</mo></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><msup><mn>2</mn><mn>2</mn></msup></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mroot><mn>16</mn><mn>3</mn></mroot></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>16</mn></mfrac><mo>&#8901;</mo><msup><mn>2</mn><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msup><mn>2</mn><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mo>.</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#8901;</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#8901;</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

</details>

### Q81. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mi>A</mi></math>, <math><mi>B</mi></math> are two non-singular matrices of order <math><mn>3</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>I</mi></math> is an identity matrix of order <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn></math> such that <math><mi>A</mi><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">Ι</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><msup><mi>A</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>A</mi><mi>T</mi></msup><mo>-</mo><mi>A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>, then <math><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math> is equal to (where <math><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow></msup></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mfenced><mi>A</mi></mfenced></math> denote transpose and adjoint matrices of the matrix <math><mi>A</mi></math> respectively)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>⋅</mo><msup><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>⋅</mo><msup><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>⋅</mo><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>⋅</mo><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>⋅</mo><msup><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></math> &hellip;.(1)
<math><mn>3</mn><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
Multiplying by <math><mi>A</mi></math> on both sides, we get
<math><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>A</mi><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mi>A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>7</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi></math>
<math><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>4</mn><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></math>
<math><msup><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></math> (using (1))
<math><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#8901;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q82. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>i</mi><mi>j</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math> be a square matrix such that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><msup><mi>A</mi><mi>T</mi></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>I</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>A</mi></mfenced><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>0</mn></math> . If <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>11</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>13</mn></mrow></msub></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>21</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>22</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>23</mn></mrow></msub></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>31</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>32</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>33</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>I</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>. Then, <math><mi>&#955;</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi>A</mi><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>I</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>I</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>64</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></math> or <math><mn>8</mn></math>
As, <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>11</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>13</mn></mrow></msub></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>21</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>22</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>23</mn></mrow></msub></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>31</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>32</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>33</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>I</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>A</mi><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>I</mi><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>I</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>I</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q83. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The number of triplets <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>b</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> of positive integers satisfying the equation <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>b</mi></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>c</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>a</mi><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>c</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>a</mi><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><mi>b</mi><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>30</mn></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>3</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>6</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>9</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>12</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mn>3</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>b</mi></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>c</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>a</mi><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>c</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>a</mi><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><mi>b</mi><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>30</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfrac></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfrac></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>30</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>30</mn></math>
Apply <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>R</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>&#8596;</mo><msub><mi>R</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>R</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>R</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>30</mn></math>
Apply <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>C</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>&#8596;</mo><msub><mi>C</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>C</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>C</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>&#8596;</mo><msub><mi>C</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>C</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>30</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>30</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>29</mn></math>
<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>b</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8801;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1,1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> or <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1,3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> or <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3,1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

</details>

### Q84. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The value of <math><mo>∆</mo><mi> </mi><mo>=</mo><mi> </mi><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd><mtd><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd><mtd><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>6</mn><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd><mtd><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>4</mn><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mrow></mtd><mtd><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>9</mn><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd><mtd><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mn>0</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Multiplying <math><msub><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> by <math><mn>3</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></math> <math><mi>&#952;</mi></math> and dividing <math><mo>&#8710;</mo></math> by <math><mn>3</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></math> <math><mi>&#952;</mi></math>, we get, 

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8710;</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi>&#160;</mi><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#952;</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#952;</mi></mtd><mtd><msup><mi>sin</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#952;</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#952;</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>&#952;</mi></mtd><mtd><msup><mi>sin</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#952;</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#952;</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>9</mn><mi>&#952;</mi></mtd><mtd><msup><mi>sin</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#952;</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>

<math><mo>&#8757;</mo><mn>3</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></math>

Now, applying <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>C</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>&#8596;</mo><msub><mi>C</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><msub><mi>C</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></math></p><p><math><msub><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> becomes identical with <math><msub><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> </p><p>Hence, <math><mo>&#8710;</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q85. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>a</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>b</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>c</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>a</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo> </mo><mo>∀</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>∈</mo><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>. If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi></math> is a singular matrix, then the number of possible matrices <math><mi>A</mi></math> are

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>18</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>27</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>7</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>3</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mn>7</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> (for a singular matrix)
<math><mo>⇒</mo><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi></math>
<math><mn>7</mn></math> cases <math><mfenced close="" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mrow><maligngroup></maligngroup><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>2</mn></mrow></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mrow><maligngroup></maligngroup><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mrow><maligngroup></maligngroup><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mrow><maligngroup></maligngroup><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi></mi><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi></mi><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
Hence, total number of possible matrices <math><mo>=</mo><mn>7</mn></math>

</details>

### Q86. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If the matrix <math><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>5</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>, then the value of <math><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>100</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>98</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>20</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>18</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>3</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>1</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>5</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>100</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>98</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>20</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>18</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>98</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>I</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>18</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>I</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>80</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

</details>

### Q87. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Consider three square matrices <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>B</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>C</mi></math> of order <math><mn>3</mn></math> such that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>A</mi><mi>T</mi></msup><mo>=</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>B</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>B</mi><mi>T</mi></msup><mo>=</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>C</mi></math>, then the incorrect option is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>C</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>A</mi><mi>T</mi></msup><mo>=</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>B</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>A</mi><mi>T</mi></msup></math> </p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>B</mi></math> is a skew symmetric matrix of order <math><mn>3</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>B</mi><mi>T</mi></msup><mo>=</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>C</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>C</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>B</mi><mi>T</mi></msup></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>C</mi></math> is a skew symmetric matrix of order <math><mn>3</mn><mi></mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
Taking transpose of first equation
<math><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow></msup><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow></msup></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>B</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>B</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mi>C</mi></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>C</mi></math></p>

</details>

### Q88. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>5</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>6</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>4</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>5</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>6</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> then the determinant value of <math><mi>B</mi><mi>A</mi></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>8</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>24</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>0</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi>B</mi><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>4</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>5</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>6</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mi></mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>5</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>6</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>17</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>22</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>27</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>22</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>29</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>36</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>27</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>36</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>45</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>17</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>5</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>5</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>22</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>7</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>7</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>27</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>9</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>9</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math> (Using <math><msub><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>→</mo><msub><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>→</mo><msub><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> )
Hence, <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

</details>

### Q89. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>i</mi><mi>j</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></math> be a <math><mn>3</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>3</mn></math> matrix where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>a</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mi>j</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>i</mi><mi>j</mi></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>j</mi><mi>i</mi></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>i</mi><mi>j</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>i</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mi>j</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>i</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mi>j</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>i</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>j</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable><mo>,</mo></mrow></mfenced></math>
then the minimum value of <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to (where <math><mi>x</mi></math> is a real number)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>33</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>7</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>33</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>33</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>11</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>13</mn></mrow></msub></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>21</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>22</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>23</mn></mrow></msub></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>31</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>32</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>33</mn></mrow></msub></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>  
<math><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>11</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>13</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>21</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>  
<math><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>22</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>23</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>11</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>31</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>32</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>33</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>  
<math><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>11</mn><mi>x</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>33</mn><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi></math>  
Minimum value of <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>33</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

---

### Q90. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>b</mi><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>c</mi></math> are sides of the triangle <math><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mi>C</mi></math> and <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>a</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>b</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>c</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>a</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>b</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>c</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0,</mn></math> then the value of <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>A</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>B</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>C</mi></mrow></mrow></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>By expansion of the determinant, we get,
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>a</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>b</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>c</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>a</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>b</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>c</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>c</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>c</mi><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>c</mi><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>which is true for <math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>c</mi></math></p><p>Hence, <math><mo>&#8710;</mo><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mi>C</mi></math> must be an equilateral triangle
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>C</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mn>60</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></msup></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>A</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>B</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>C</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math></p>

</details>

### Q91. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>d</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> be an identity in <math><mi>x</mi></math> where <math><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>b</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>c</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>d</mi></math> are independent of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo></math> then the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>13</mn><mn>25</mn></mfrac><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi><mi>d</mi></math> is

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **24.96**

**Detailed Solution:**

Since, the given equation is an identity in <math><mi>x</mi></math>, putting <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>, we get, 
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi><mi>d</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi><mi>d</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>48</mn><mspace linebreak="newline"/><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mn>13</mn><mn>25</mn></mfrac><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi><mi>d</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>13</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>48</mn></mrow><mn>25</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>24.96</mn></math>

</details>

### Q92. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#945;</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>5</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mi>&#945;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Q</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn><mi>l</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mi>m</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>5</mn><mi>n</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math> are two matrices, where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>l</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>m</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>n</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>R</mi></math>, then the value of determinant <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mi>Q</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(D)** not possible

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mn>0</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mi>Q</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>l</mi><mi>&#945;</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>m</mi><mi>&#945;</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>10</mn><mi>n</mi><mi>&#945;</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>10</mn><mi>l</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>m</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>25</mn><mi>n</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>l</mi><msup><mi>&#945;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn><mi>m</mi><msup><mi>&#945;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>15</mn><mi>n</mi><msup><mi>&#945;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>P</mi><mi>Q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>4</mn><mi>l</mi><mi>&#945;</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>m</mi><mi>&#945;</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>10</mn><mi>n</mi><mi>&#945;</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>10</mn><mi>l</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>m</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>25</mn><mi>n</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>l</mi><msup><mi>&#945;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mtd><mtd><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>m</mi><msup><mi>&#945;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>15</mn><mi>n</mi><msup><mi>&#945;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>l</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mi>m</mi></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>n</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn><mi>&#945;</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#945;</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#945;</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>5</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>5</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mi>&#945;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mtd><mtd><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mi>&#945;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mi>&#945;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
Here, <math><msub><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math> are identical
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>P</mi><mi>Q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

### Q93. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The number of values of <math><mi>α</mi></math> in <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn><mi>π</mi><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>10</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> for which the equations <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>cos</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>cos</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>cos</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> have nontrivial solution is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>10</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>20</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>40</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>15</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>20</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

For non-trivial solution <math><mo>&#8710;</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd><mtd><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mrow></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>14</mn><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mspace linebreak="newline"/><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi>n</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>I</mi></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo></math> Number of values of <math><mi>&#945;</mi></math> in <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>10</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is <math><mn>20</mn></math>

</details>

### Q94. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>   2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>   0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>   3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>   3</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>   1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math> be a matrix, then <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>&nbsp&nbsp&nbsp2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>&nbsp&nbsp&nbsp0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>&nbsp&nbsp&nbsp3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>&nbsp&nbsp&nbsp3</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>&nbsp&nbsp&nbsp1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>&nbsp&nbsp&nbsp1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>&nbsp&nbsp&nbsp3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>&nbsp&nbsp&nbsp0</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>&nbsp&nbsp&nbsp2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>&nbsp&nbsp&nbsp2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>&nbsp&nbsp&nbsp0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>&nbsp&nbsp&nbsp3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>&nbsp&nbsp&nbsp3</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>&nbsp&nbsp&nbsp1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>?</mo></math>

<math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>.</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math>
