**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>…</mo><mo>…</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>53</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

### Q96. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> be a square matrix of order <math><mn>3</mn></math>, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></msup></math> be the transpose matrix of matrix <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> and <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></math>. If <math><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>, then the value of <math><mn>12</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi></math> is equal to <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **96**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mfenced><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>&#8230;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8757;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>64</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mfenced><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
From <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></msup><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></msup><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>12</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>96</mn></math></p>
